1b1(rgyud): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya pany+tsa ping 
1b2(rgyud): sha/ta padz+nyA pA ra mi tA mu kha nA ma ma ha yA 
1b3(rgyud): na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags pa shes rab 
2a1(rgyud): @##//kyi pha rol tu phyin pa sgo nyid shu rtsa lnga pa zhes bya 
2a2(rgyud): ba theg pa chen po'i mdo//sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems 
2a3(rgyud): dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//'di skad bdag 
2a4(rgyud): gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das ri rab kyi zom 
2b1(rgyud): la lha thams cad kyi mtshan mo'i gnas na /byang chub sems dpa' 
2b2(rgyud): bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phag dum dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/
2b3(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das lha dang lha'i bu dang /lha ma yin dang /
2b4(rgyud): rlung lha dang /namakha' lding dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /mi 'am 
2b5(rgyud): ci dang /srin po dang /rigs sngags 'chang thams cad dang /
3a1(rgyud): @##//'byung po dang /log 'den dang /sha za dang /
3a2(rgyud): snang ba dang /mi snang ba'i ma mo'i tshogs shin tu mang zhing rgya che ba dpag 
3a3(rgyud): tshad lta'i dkyila 'khor du 'dus pa sha stag dang /dge 
3a4(rgyud): slong stong nyis brgya lnga bcus bskor cing mdun du bdar nas 
3a5(rgyud): chos ston te/de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
3a6(rgyud): chen po la lag na rdo rje/rdo rje'i rigs thams cad kyis yongs su 
3b1(rgyud): bskor cing rig pa'i tshogs dang /phyag rgya'i tshogs de dag thams cad kyis rig sngags 'chang bye ba phrag du 
3b2(rgyud): mas yongs su bskor nas/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba der song ste phyin nas bar snang las babs nas/
3b3(rgyud): bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das la lan gsum bskor ba 
3b4(rgyud): byas nas/phyogs gcig tu 'dug pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis 
3b5(rgyud): gsang ba'i bdag po lag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa/gsang ba'i bdag po nyi shu rtsa lnga 
3b6(rgyud): po 'di rnams ni nga'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo yin no//oM sangs rgyas dang byang chub 
3b7(rgyud): sems dpa' thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo//oM byang chub kyi sems rdo rje shes rab kyi pha rol tu/
4a1(rgyud): @#//phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM kun tu bzang po spyod pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM yid bzhin gyi nor bu/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM 'gog pa med pa shes 
4a2(rgyud): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'isgo'o//oM skye bar 'gyur ba/shes rab kyi pha rol tu/phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM rnam par shes pa thaMd/shes rab kyi pha rol tu yin pa'i sgo'o//oM 'bar ba chen po'i chos 
4a3(rgyud): nyid/shes rab kyi pha rol tuphyin pa'i sgo'o//oM brtson 'grus shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa'i sgo'o//oM thams cad du 'gro ba shes rab kyi pha rol tu byin pa'i sgo'o//oM rdo rje thaMd brtan 
4a4(rgyud): pa'i sems/shes rab kyi pha rol tu sbyin pa'i sgo'o//oM de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd/shes rab kyi phyin du phyin pa'i sgo'o//o~M rang bzhin rnam par dag pa'i chos nyid yais 
4a5(rgyud): rnam par dag pa/shes rab kyi pha rol duphyin pa'i sgo'o//oM las rnam par sbyong ba/shes rab kyi pha rol du ma phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM nges par gnod mdzes rdo rje can phar/shes rab kyi pha rol tu
4a6(rgyud): pa'i sgo'o//oM 'dod pa la 'dod chags rnam par dag pa/shes rabagyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM mgyog+sa pa'i rdo rje can/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo'o //oM hUM thaMd du 'gro ba/shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyin 
4a7(rgyud): pa'i sgo'o//oM hrIHshes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin/pa'i sgo'o//oM a zhes bya ba'i sgo'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa'i sgo'o//oM shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa/shes rab kyi pha rolata phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM aM ba hUM/shes 
4a8(rgyud): rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o //oM e shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pa'i sgo'o//oM de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku'i mchog//shes rab kyi pha rolatu phyinapa'i sgo//oM de bzhin gsheg pa thaMd kyi gsung 
4b1(rgyud): rnam par dag pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgo'o//oM de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi thugs kyi rdo rje/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i sgo'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/
4b2(rgyud): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po lag na rdo rje dang /thaMd dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dang /lha dang /mi dang lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yi rangs bcom ldan 'das gyis gsungs 
4b3(rgyud): pa la mngon par bstod do////'phags pa shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa'i sgo nyi shu rtsa lnga pa ces bye ba/theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogas+ho////rgya gar skadu/sarba ta thA ga ta kA ya bAkat+ti gu h+ya a lang kA ra
4b4(rgyud): byA ho tan+t+ra rA dza nA mA/bod skad du/de bzhin gshegs bsku dang gsung dang thugs kyis gsang ba rgyan gyi bkod ces bya ba'i rgyud kyi rgyal po/bla ma dang 'jam pa'i dbyangs kyi zhabs kyi pad+mo la gus phyag 'tshalo//'di skad
4b5(rgyud): bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje yais rnal+yor gyi dbang phyug chen po dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thaMdakyi sku dang 
4b6(rgyud): gsung dang thugs rdo+e yais phyag rgya chen po'i dam tshig byin gyis brlabs kyi rdo rje'i sku chen po dang /de bzhin/gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku dngagasung dang thugs rdo rje yes rin po che chen po'i cod pan gyi rtse mo'i 
4b7(rgyud): gtsug phud kyis dbang bskur ba brnyed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs thaMd kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje yais kyi tinge 'dzin chen po dang ldan pa dang /de bzhin gsheDa pa thaMd kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs rdo+e yai kyi las snogs pa 
4b8(rgyud): chen po dang /de bzhin gshags pa thaMd kyi sku dang gsang dang thugs rdo rje yai mchod pa chen po spro ba'i dam tshigi gsang ba dang /de bzhin gsheDa thaMd kyi sku dang gsung dag thugs ye shes dkyil 'khor 
5a1(rgyud): @#//chen po dam tshig gi de kho na nyid/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shes gsang ba chen po'i snying po dang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams 
5a2(rgyud): kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs/rdo rje ye shes kyi mnga' bdag chen po dang //de bzhin gshes pa thams cad kyi sku dag gsung dang thugs rdo rje yi shes kun tu bzang po dang //de bzhin 
5a3(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad kyis sku dang gsung dang thugs dang rje ye shes kun tu bzang po dag gi sku dang gsung dang thugs
5a4(rgyud): dang /rigs pa gnyis su med par 'gyur ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa tham cad kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje ye shes pha rol tu phyin pa'i snying po la dga' ba dang /de bzhin gshe 
5a5(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi sku dang /gsung dang thugs rdo rje ye shas kyi lhag pa 'i snying po mdzes pa dang /de bzhin gshegs thams cad kyi sku dang gsang dang thugs rdo rje ye she sa rgyan chen po bkod pa'i 
5a6(rgyud): dkyil 'khor chen po'i snying po la nam mkha'i khams thams cad kyi rdul phra rab kyi rdul dang mnyam pa'i rdo rje ye shes chen po 'ba' zhig dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs so//de'ang 'di lta ste/sgo mtha' 
5a7(rgyud): yas pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yum chen mo dang /nam mkha' sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yum can mo dang /a po dza'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yul chen mo dang /'gyur ba med pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje 
5a8(rgyud): shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin ma zhes bya ba'i rdo rje yum chan mo dang /gzhan yang ma lus shing lus pa med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rdul phra rab dang mnyam pa'i brtson 'grus dang ldan pa'i rdo rje btsun mo 
5b1(rgyud): stong phrag brgya la'ang 'di lta ste/mda'i mtshon cha'i lag ces bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo dang /dbang phyug la lta ba'i rim zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo ni/nye bar zhi ba'i rang bzhin ras zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun ma dang /gyen du 
5b2(rgyud): lta ba'i bzhin ras zhes bya ba'i btsun mo dang /'dod zla mtshan rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun po dang /brda' sprod pa ma zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsunamo dang /'bri ba'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje btsun pa dang /rol pa dang ldan pa zhes 
5b3(rgyud): bya ba'i rdo rje btsun mo dang/'dod pa'i mchog ces bya ba'i rdo rje btsunamo ma dang /dbang phyug gis bde ba'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje'i btsun mo dang /dmigs pa'i sgo zhes bya ba'i rdo rje batshun mo dang /brang las skyes zhes bya ba'i rdo 
5b4(rgyud): rje btsun mo ste/de dag la sogs pa rdo rje btsun mo rnams kyis yong su bskor cing mdun du bdar to//gzhan yang dpag tu med pa'i rdo rje pho nya mo chen mo rnams dang /thabs gcig tu bzhugs te/
5b5(rgyud): de yang 'di lta ste/snying bskul bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje pho nya mo chen po dang /gdug pa thams cad 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje phonya mo chen mo dang /rjes su mnyes pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i rdo rje pho ྅ 
5b6(rgyud): mo chen mo dang /khrag 'gugs pa zhes bya ba'i rdor rje pho nya mo chen mo dang /thams cad 'phrog pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje phau nya mo chen mo la sogs pa'o//gzhan yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgyas pa'i rdo rje sgo srung ma chen mo rnams 
5b7(rgyud): dang /yang thabs gcig te/de'ang 'di lta ste/rdo rje 'bebs pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje sgo srung ma chenamo dang /dam tshig gsum ldan ces bya ba'i rdo rje sgo srungs ma chen mo dang /'jig pa'i yan lag can ces bya ba'i rdo rje 
5b8(rgyud): sgo srung ma chen mo dang /thog ma pa zhes bya ba'i rdo rje sgor srung ma chen mo dang /gdul dka' 'dul ces bya ba'i rdo rje sgo srug ma chen mo la sogs pa'o//gzhan yang mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa'i rdo rje bran mo chen mo rnams 
6a1(rgyud): @#//
6b1(rgyud): zhes bya ba dang /rdo rje me dang /rdo rje mi 'khrugs pa dang /rdo rje chu dang /gzhan yang rdo rje rgyal po chen po bzhi la 'di lta ste/zhi bar byed pa dang /rgyas par byed pa dang /dbang du byed pa dang /mi shes pa thams cad zad par byed pa ste de dag la sogs 
6b2(rgyud): pa'o//gzhan yang mchog tu rgya chen po sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rab tu gnas pa'i mchod pa dang ldan pa bcom ldan 'das ston pa rtag pa mchog gi mnga' bdag ste/kun tu bzang po'i sku yis ni//'jig rten gsum 
6b3(rgyud): gyi dkyil 'khor spros//bcom ldan rdo rje 'dzin leg bzhugs//gsang ba rgyan gyi sbyor bas so//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad bskyed par byed pa'i yum gsang ba'i rdo rje 'dus pa thob ste/de bzhin gshegs 
6b4(rgyud): pa thams cad kyi yang dang lhan cig tu dga' ba'i bde ba rjes su byung ste/'khor dang bcas pa rnams bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar lhags te/rdo rje'i glu 'dis mngon par bstod nas zhu ba zhus pa/
6b5(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun don khyod phyag 'tshal/rdo rje 'byung ba khyod la 'dud//thugs kyi rin chen mchog khyod 'dud//chos kyi blo gros khyod la 'dud//chos rnams kun mchog khyod la 'dud//sna tshogs 
6b6(rgyud): dgongs pa khyod la 'dud//dam tshig mchog gi khyod la 'dud//rdo rje sems dpa' phyags 'tshal 'dud//grags pa chen po bdag cag ni//mgon po khyod la zhu ba zhu//sangs rgyas thams cad bskyed byed yum//dkyil 'khor 
6b7(rgyud): gyi ni dkyil 'khor mchog/de nas bcom ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' des de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje'i yum rnams la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//de bzhin gshegs pa thams kyi yum rnams de skad 
6b8(rgyud): ma smra gcig/'jig rten thams cad la khyab pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po chen po 'di la ma 'ongs pa'i sems can rnams dad par mi 'gyur zhing //rang gi sems yongs su sun 'byin la de ma mthong ba'i chos la ma dad pas rang 
7a1(rgyud): gi sems yang dag par sun phyung ste/mnar med pa rnamasu skyes nas bskal pa stong phrag mang por mnar med pa'i sdug bsngal rjesu 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de rgya lam na mnar med pa'i sdug
7a2(rgyud): bsngal las grol te/mi rnamasu skyes par gyur kyang rnam par 'gyur ba'i bzhin du 'gyuro//rjes ngas pa rnams dang /blo gros chung ba rnams dang /khyi dang /bya rog dang /byi la dang /phag dang /stag dang /glang po che dang /seng+ge'i
7a3(rgyud): nga ro 'dra ba dag tu 'gyuro//de dang 'dre bar mi bya zhing blta bar mi bya'o//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd bskyed pa'i yum de rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das ji ltar sems
7a4(rgyud): can de rnams kyi mnar med pa'i sdug bsngal+za zad par 'gyur/bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsal pa/gang gis 'di'i ming gzung nas yongasu zad par 'gyuro//bsod nams de'i rgyus de'i las kyi sgrib
7a5(rgyud): pa zad de bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi skal ba can du 'gyuro//de nas de dag yid dga' ba skyes nas yang bcom ldan 'das la 'di sad ces gsol to//bcom
7a6(rgyud): bstan du gsol/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyi rgyus dkyil 'khor chen po'i rgyal po sems can thaMd la phan pa dang bde ba'i phyir gang ma 'ongs pa'i sems can rnams kyis dran pa tsam gyi sangs
7a7(rgyud): rgyas kyi byang chub thobs par 'gyuro//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis kun tu bzang po chen po 'od 'phro ba gsal ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugaso//rang gi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje rnams la
7a8(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku'i dam pa 'di bka' stsal pa/dkyil 'khor kun gyis yum gyur pa//stong nyid ye shes nyidu bshad//ci zhig nga yis bstan par bya//rtogs pa thaMd rnam par spangs//
7b1(rgyud): sems can rnams la phan bzhed pa//gang zhig cung zad bstan par bya//de kun sgyu mas sgyu ma'o//'gro ba sgyu ma lta bur shes//des na sgyu ma lta bu yis//sgyu ma lta bu'i dkyil 'khor bya//nam mkha'i khams kyis 
7b2(rgyud): khyab pa yis//thaMd yum gyis yang dag 'khrugs/phyi rol yang ni brtag par bya//gang pa'i dkyil 'khor yum rnams kyis/dben zhing dga' ba'i gnasu ni//yid kyi leg par 'dus byas pa//nam mkha' la ni thig 
7b3(rgyud): gis gdab//thog mar de yi dam pa so//de nas rnal 'byor chen pos thig/gsang ba'i yum gyi dkyil 'khor ro//mu khyud dag gi rnam bdun du//kun nas yongasu bskor bar bya//dbus kyis cha ru dkyil 'khor ni//
7b4(rgyud): 'khor lo lta bur bri bar bya//kun nas brgyad gnyis mdzes pa ni//dkyil 'khor bzhi yi dbus su'o//'khor lo'i lte ba'i dbusu ni//pad+ma ze 'bru ldan pa bri//mtshams dang mtshamasu 'dab ma ni//
7b5(rgyud): ji lta ba bzhin bsnyems bcas pas//srad bu lnga yis yang dag 'khrugs//srad bu bzhi yis nye bar spras//sgro bzhi dang ni yang dag ldan//rta babs seng ge dag gis brgyan//gdugs dang rgyal 
7b6(rgyud): mtshan ba dan sogs//bla res steng du bres pa dang //rin chen rdo rje pad+ma ldan sngon//dpag bsam shing gi lcug mas khyab//buM bzang rin chen ldan pa'i mchog/rgyan 'phreng glang po ldan pa'i ni/phyi rol gnas te legs 
7b7(rgyud): par bri//rtog dang mi rtog rnam spangs te//dkyil 'khor chen po'i dbusu ni//gsang ba rgyan gyi rdo rje bya//mnyam par bzhag cing spro ba yis/sems kyis bri ba nyid bya//de nas lha mo zhal bzhi ma//shes rab 
7b8(rgyud): pha rol phyin dge ba//phyag bzhi pa la spyan yangs ma//phyag gnyis kyis ni chos 'chad cing //zhi ba'i g.yas par rdo rje ste//g.yon pa pad+mas rnam par brgyan//dkar dang dmar po char sngo ba//sngo bsangs rin chen 
8a1(rgyud): mchog mdzes 'od//gtsug na rin chen gtsug tor che//gtsug rgyan cod pan dam pa can//skal ba chen po 'od ldan mo//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par klubs//gos kyis lto ni legs 
8a2(rgyud): bsgribs pa//bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor 'dzum bcas shing //sna tshogs gos ni legs gyon pa//dbu ma'i zhal ras gser gyi 'od//zhabs gnyis dbusu 'khor lo ste//mdun na rin chen bdun gnas pa//pad+ma 
8a3(rgyud): mchog ldan seng+ge'i khri//zla ba rdo rje 'dzin pa'i gdan//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'od 'phro ba//rgyab du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//dpa' bo de yis bri bar bya//'di yi mdun du bi bya ba//dpa' bo mtha' yas pa yi zhal//
8a4(rgyud): stong phrag du ma'i phyag ldan ma//mtshan nyid thaMd yang dag rdzogs//rgyan rnams kun gyis rna mar brgyan//phyag gi rdo rje pad+ma 'chang //sna tshogs phyogs rgyas phyag gzhan dkrigs//gtsug 
8a5(rgyud): ldan dar gyi cod pan can//dbu brgyan pad+ma'i bzhin ras ma//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dag la bzhugs//de 'od nyi ma lta bu'i 'od//dpa' bo'i rnal 'byor ldan pa yi//dpa' bo des ni bri bar bya//
8a6(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' bdag sgom te//der ni snyems pas 'gro bar bya//dpa' bos lho ru bri bya ba//gshen rje'i 'dab ma mdzes pa la//mchog sbyin namakha' dge ma yang //rin chen khri la legs par bzhugs//
8a7(rgyud): bzhin ras sngo la spyan dkar ba//'dzum bcas nor bu'i 'og pag can//mgul rgyan ri chen phreng ba mchog/rkang gdub gdu bu rin po che//yid bzhin nor bu'i lag pa can//sbyin pa'i pha rol phyin dgongs 
8a8(rgyud): nam//de nas blo ldan rab song ste//nub tu yang ni de bzhin du//dpal dang ldan pas legs par bri//chu las skyed ma yid 'phrog ma//dmar po'i gos 'dzin dag pa ma//gtsug na cod pan gyis spras ma//
8b1(rgyud): sbrul rgyan sna tshogs lus can ma//dar dang rin chen gyis klubs na//pad+ma 'byed pa'i sbyor ba yis//phyag gi lo 'dab dang ldan pas//zla ba'i gdan la yang dag bzhugs//nyi ma du ma'i snang ba can//ji ltar rigs pa 
8b2(rgyud): bri bar bya//de nas byang du phyin nas su//song ste dang 'a bos bri bya ba//mi g.yo bzhin ras grags chen ma//mar gad 'bar ba mtshungs pa mo//dkar po'i dar gyis yang dag klubs//gtsug na nor bu cod pan bzang //
8b3(rgyud): gtsug rgyan sna tshogs dag gis spras//rdo rje dril bu'i lag bkra ma//sna tshogs rgyan dang gos ldan pas//zla ba'i ba dan la yang dag bzhugs/'od kyis gtams pa'i dkyil 'khor mchog/de nas 'tshams
8b4(rgyud): kyi 'dab ma la//me tog bdug pa dri ldan ma//rdo rje snang byed dag pa ma//mchod pa sngags rig pa y+si bris//gzugs sgra dri dang ro dang reg/rang gi bdag po'i phyogasu'o//'dab ma 'dab mar thaMd
8b5(rgyud): du//bzhad dang sgreg dang dbyangs chen mo//gar gyi phyag rgya grags chen po//sngags pas 'bad de bri bar bya//pad+ma las skyes 'dab ma la//thaMd du ni 'grub pa bri//sgo rnams dag ni
8b6(rgyud): thaMd la//sgo skyong ba ni rnam bzhi dang //phyi rol du yang lha la sogs//ming ni yang dag bri bar bya//de nas khrus byas lus byugs la//me tog bdug sogs kyis bdug ste//sna tshogs gos ni rab gyon la//
8b7(rgyud): me tog phreng dang gos gyis brgyan//gtsug phud cod pan dang ldan zhing //mdzes pa'i dar gyis yang dag klubs//rdo rje dril bu lag par ldan//sbyor ba bzang ba'i rnam gzhag gis//de nas thaMd dgug par bya//rdo rje ma mo dka'
8b8(rgyud): thub che//bzlog dang rnal ma'i phyag rgya y+si//rdo rje sems dpa' bzlas nasu /rdo rje'i sbyor bas gar bya zhing //lan gsum du ni g.yas bskor bas//rdo rje ma mo'i dkyil 'khor rnams//dam tshig ldan pa thaMd byon//glu dbyangs
9a1(rgyud): @#//kyis ni rgyal pos bskul//rang gi slob ma'i tshogs gar gyis//dreg pa yis ni g.yas dang g.yon//'gying ba dang ni 'dud par bya//'dir gshegs 'dir shegs seMn don mdzad brtson pa po//ser sna 
9a2(rgyud): mi mnga' sangs rgyas tshogs mchog mtha' dag dang //bde gshegs dam pa'i yum gyur srid pa'i bskyed pa po//pha rol don mdzad dus la di ring bab pa lags//ces so de nas de dag rang nyid kyi//rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul
9a3(rgyud): pas byon//mtshan ma dag ni bzang po rnams//dkyil 'khor dag tu blta bar bya//rang ngam gzhan la bab sogs pa'i//mtshan ma ji ltar mthong ba na//de tshe nges par ma mo ni//bde gshegs thugs kyi dgong par byon//de nas mchod
9a4(rgyud): yon zhabs bsil cho ga ji bzhin rab brtags te//de tshe babs pa'i seMn bsod nams ldan la bri bar bya//gal te de ni mi smra ba na/hUM gi yi ge dang //ldan pa'i glu dbyangs kyis ni yongasu bskul ba nyidu bya//de nas
9a5(rgyud): shes rab ldan ma bzhugs//slob ma'i gtso bo thal sbyar te//mig la mig dar gyis g.yogs la//rab dga' bdag yin yang dag dga'//ma mo thaMd la dmigs te//der ni me tog dor bar bya//me 
9a6(rgyud): tog gang la lhung gyur pa//ma mo da ni de yis 'grub//de nas go rims ji bzhin du//sgo skyong ba nas brtsams nasu//shes rab pha rol phyin pa'i bar//slob ma la ni bshad par bya//dam tshig thob par gyur nas su//sdom pa 
9a7(rgyud): dag ni bzung bar bya//ma mo'i sngag dang phyag rgya ni//mnyam par bzhag pas stan par bya//gzhan du slob dpon blo ngan ni//nges par dmyal bar 'dod par 'gyur//des kyang yang dag de 'grub cing //myur du dmyal bar 'gro bar 
9a8(rgyud): 'gyur//bgegs dang log par 'dren pa 'am//rims dang gdon dang dug sogs kyis//gdungs te nad kyis rgyun du ni//'chi ba ru yang 'gyur ba yin//rdo rje slob dpon smad pa na//rtag tu dmyal ba nyid du ni//rab tu 
9b1(rgyud): smongs pa'i slob ma 'gro//mkha' las rdo rje ji bzhin no//des na bu kye dam tshig ni//khyod kyis rtag tu srung bar byos//ma mo kun gyi sdom pa ni//theg pa chen po'i cho gas gnas//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis 
9b2(rgyud): ting nge 'dzin la bzhugs te/rdo rje 'dzin pa thaMd kyis yum rnams kyi dam tshig la bka' stsal pa//rin chen sems kyi dam tshig gis/rnam pa gsum ni sbyang bar bya//thog mtha' bar gyi sbyor ba yis//chung 'bring 
9b3(rgyud): mchog gi dbye ba yis//de 'dra'i dam tshig yang dag pa//sangs rgyas kun gyis bka' stsal to//ji srid sems ni sbyang kyi bar//'bad pa yis ni bsgom par bya//bud med thaMd mthong ba ni//lan gsum du ni bskor nas kyang //
9b4(rgyud): spyi bor phyag ni byas nas su//lan gsum bar du gsol ba btab//bdag gi ma khyod bu yin te//ji srid byang chub thob bar du//byang chub yan lag nu ma la//byung ma rang gi 'o mas skyongs//de nas sbyor ldan rab dge 
9b5(rgyud): ba'i//dbang skur ba ni sbyin par bya//gser la sogs pa'i bum pa ru//dri yis chu yi yongs bkang la//dar dang ras sogs gis g.yogs te//me tog chun pos mgul rgyan nas//rin chen 
9b6(rgyud): sman gyis rab bkang ba'i//gcig lhag bcu drug bzang ba la//ma mo'i snying po nyid kyis ni//brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas pa yis//sngags shes pas ni dbang bskur ro//slob ma zhi ba bzang po yis//thog mar zhu ba byas 
9b7(rgyud): nas kyang //zhabs gnyis la ni mgos btud nas//bdag ni che ge mo rdo rje//byang chub sems ni rnam sgom pa'o//slob dpon zhabs kyi chu skyes la//dbang bskur phyir ni zhu ba bgyid//bskal pa chen po bdag la stsol//slob 
9b8(rgyud): dpon mchog tu rmad byung ba//dbang bskur rgyal ba thams cad kyi//sangs rgyas byang chub rab sgrub pa//des ni bdag gi sems can rnams//don gyi dam pa bdag gis bgyi//sangs rgyas kun gyi don bgyid pa//
10a1(rgyud): @#//brtul zhugs bzang po khyod kyi drin//de nas dbang skur sbyor ba yis//rdo rje sems dpa'i gong 'phang gnas//slob ma ye shes rdo rje yi//go 'phangs la ni bzhag nas su//bum pa bzang 
10a2(rgyud): po gcig gam ni//yang na cu drug dag gam ni//lnga 'am yang na bcu yis ni//dkyil 'khor du ni dbang bskur ro//dkyil 'khor gyi mdun du ni//dkyil 'khor pad+ma bri bar bya//pad+ma lo 'dab brgyad pa la//ge ser lte ba 
10a3(rgyud): ldan pa bri//de yi dbus su rdo rje bri//sna mtshogs rdo rje 'od zer phreng //de yi mdun du 'khor lo bri//chen po rin chen bdun gyis bskor//lho yi phyogs su rin po che//nub tu pad+ma nyid yin te//byang du ral gri chen po'o//'bar 
10a4(rgyud): ba'i phreng 'khrugs 'od zer can//grwa yi phyogs ni thams cad du//me tog bdug pa dri zhim pa//'od ldan pa ni skal ba che//sgo dang sgo ru bzhi nyid do//phyi rol du yang mi 'chi kun//gcig nas gcig tu 
10a5(rgyud): rgyal sgrogs pa//bde byed gcir bu'i cha lugs te//ming thogs pa dang ldan pa'o//dkyil 'khor de yi dbus su ni//slob ma bsnyems dang bcas pa bzhag/chos kyi rang bzhin gtam brjod de//
10a6(rgyud): de ring khyod ni rigs su skyes//'khor lo sa bsgyur ba'i rgyal po chen//rtag pa dam pa'i dbang phyug pa//thabs dang shes rab ldan pa'i dpal//dag pa'i dbang phyug kun gyi bdag/rdo rje pad+ma lag byin te//gnang ba bzang 
10a7(rgyud): po rab tu sbyin//bu yi sa bya ba chen po gyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi go 'phangs gnas//der ni sbyor ba 'ongs nas su//gsang ba'i sdom pa sbyin par bya//'di ni mchog tu dga' ba ste//yum rnams rab tu rmad byung pa'o//
10a8(rgyud): dus su 'dod pa nyid du gyis//dam tshig mchog tu gyur pa'o//brtul zhugs bzang pos ci 'byor pas//de nas yon ni phul byas nas//slob dpon gyi ni zhabs la btud//ma dang chung ma bu mo dang //sring mo sring 
10b1(rgyud): mo'i bu mo dang //bu rnams dang ni bdag nyid dang //rgyal po longs spyod gter dang ni//nor dang 'bru dang grong shing rta//glang po rta dang ba glang sogs//ma he dang ni ma he mo//gzhan dang gzhan yang ci 'dod pa//bla ma la ni chen 
10b2(rgyud): pos dbus//bya dang mi bya rdzas kyi mchog/thaMd kyang ni dbul bar bya//de nas bcom ldan mi rnams la//rgyan phreng rnams dang bzhad gad dang //sgeg dang rtse 'jo dga' ba dang //rab tu dga' ba'i bde chen dang //
10b3(rgyud): bza' btung bca' la sogs pa//rnam pa du ma thaMd kyis//mchod nas gshegasu gsol bar bya//seMn kun gyi don dang ni//bdag gi yang ni cher ches mdzad//bde gshegs gang du gshegs pa der//ma mo 
10b4(rgyud): thaMd gshegasu gsol//shes rab ldan pas yon phul te//slob ma'i mchog dang bcas pa yis//gar byas lag pa mgor bzhag ste//rims kyis 'gyed dang 'dud par bya//de nas 
10b5(rgyud): bzang rnam gnyes te//thaMd sgrub par byed pa'i mchog/bdag gi skal bzang stsal nas kyang //rang gi sras kyi gnasu gshegs//de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje 
10b6(rgyud): yais yum chen mo rnams kyi phyag rgya ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor yongasu rdzogaso////de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd gyi gsang ba'i yum bcom ldan 'das ma rnams kyi yang gsang ba 'dus 
10b7(rgyud): pa las byung ba rang rang gi sgrub pa'i thabs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces zhuso//shes rab yais mchog sgrubs pa'i//seMs dpa' chen pos gsung du gsol//thabs kyis seMn 
10b8(rgyud): thaMd kun//sangs rgyas byang chub 'bras thob pa//rdo rje yais mchog gyur pa//rdo rje sku ste rdo rje mchog/dngos grub kun gyi bde ster ba//rdo rje chen po gsung du gsol//thugs kyi rin chen ldan pa'i mchog/
11a1(rgyud): @#//gzi brjid rdo rje snang ba can//seMn rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul dang //'grub pa'i yon+tan 'byung gsungs//sangs rgyas kun gyi rnal 'byor che//rnal 'byol chen po dbang phyug che//dpa' 
11a2(rgyud): ba'i sbyor bas gar gnas pa'i//dpa' bo chen pos gshad du gsol//las kun gyis ni 'bras bsnyes pas//sna tshogs khyen pa dbang phyug che//seMn rnams kyi yab chen po//sna tshogs dam pas bshad 
11a3(rgyud): du gsol//zhes bya bso//de nas bcom ldan rtag pa'i rgyal//dam pa'i dbang phyug kun mkhyen pas//gsung mchog namakha' dang mtshungs pa'i//zab mo yi ni chos bka' stsal//'di na sgrub po gang yang med//
11a4(rgyud): dngos grub bam ni sgrub thabs kyang //thaMd rnam par rtog las byung //rnam rtog kyang ni dmigs ma yin//bdag med sangs rgyas rnams kyang med//chos rnams med cing chos nyid med//sbyor 
11a5(rgyud): ba med cing seMn med//de bzhin pha dang ma yang med//bye ba med cing skyong ba med//'bras bu med cing rgyu yod min//lo ngas spyod med cing zab bo med//shes med shes pa po yang med//
11a6(rgyud): rnam shes med cing shes bya med//brdzun min bden pa 'ang ma yin pa//des na snang ba thaMd ni//sgyu ma lta bu nyid las byung //de ltar chos rnams rnams rig na//grol nas gos par mi 'gyur te//dngos grub chen 
11a7(rgyud): po 'grub par 'gyur//sangs rgyas byang chub bde chen po//zab mo'i chos ni thos pa na//mkhas pa gang zhig dad gyur na//snying po'i phyag rgya sngags rig rnams//gdon mi za bar 'grub par 'gyur//sbyor 
11a8(rgyud): ba 'di ni bcom ldan gsungs//thaMd dag par byed pa'i mchog/gsang ba'i rgyan la legs bzhugs nas//sems can rnams la bstan pa po//de nas yum rnams tshim gyur te//rang byung ba la zhu ba zhus//
11b1(rgyud): grub pa'i sgrub thabs rab sbyor ba//mnga' bdag rnal 'byor rnams kyis gsungs//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyis yum las kyi dam tshig 'byung ba rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting 
11b2(rgyud): nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//nam mkha'i dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//de yi steng du ma nyams pa//nam mkha'i mtha' klas khyab pa yi//phyogs kun tu ni rab 'phro 
11b3(rgyud): ba'o//de yi steng du gzi brjid che//dus mtha'i me dang mtshungs 'dra ba//de yi steng du sa chen po//gser gyi ri bo dang mtshungs pa//de yi steng du chur shes bya//zla ba gang ba kha ba 'dra//sngags pas cho ga 
11b4(rgyud): bzhin du bsam//rin chen bzhi yi rang bzhin bzang //stong phrag brgyad cu rtsa bzhi yi//dpag tshad kyi ni tshad tsam pa//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa//rta babs bzhi yi rtse mo can//ri bo chen po cog 
11b5(rgyud): brgyad pa//dbus su rdo rje'i ri bo mchog/de yi steng du rdo rje che//ku na tu phyogs pa yongs su sgom//sngo bsangs mar ga ta 'dra ba//sna tshogs 'od kyis yang dag 'khrugs//de yi steng du rdo 
11b6(rgyud): rje che//rtse mo lnga pa rnam par bsam//dkar po shel dang 'dra ba po//'od zer lnga yis rnam par spras//de yi steng du rin po che//chu skyes sngon po'i 'dab 'dra ba//sna tshogs 'od zer stong gi phreng //phyogs 
11b7(rgyud): bcu nam mkha' khyab pa'o//de yi gtsug tu pad+ma che//nam mkha' khyab pa yin ni mchog/lo ma stong phrag du ma can//lte ba dang ni ze 'brus dgrigs//de yi mdun du nyi ma che//'od zer stong phrag du ma can//de yi 
11b8(rgyud): steng du zla ba che//dkar po'i 'od zer 'bar bas dkrigs//nam mkha'i khams ni gang ba yin//gzi brjid dkar po 'phro ba'o//de yis dbus su ma mo bri//dus gsum bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas kyang //
12a1(rgyud): yongasu dag pa mkha' 'dra ma//zla ba'i dkyil 'khor 'phro ba mo//phyag gnyis ma la thugs zhi ba//bsam gtan spyan ldan bsam gtan yid//bsam gtan mchog 
12a2(rgyud): gi phyag rgya yi//ser mo gzhon nu rab bshad pa//dkar po'i gos 'dzin 'dzes pa mo//dkar po yi ni rgyan gyis brgyan//ral pa'i thor tshugs cod pan bzang //gtsug gi nor bu 'od ldan pa//
12a3(rgyud): yais gdan la mnyam par bzhugs//ba spu rnams kyi rtse bu gnas//rgyal ba rab tu 'phros pa ste//byang chub pha rol son par mdzad//de yi thugs kar rnam bsam pa//yi ge mchog tu mi 
12a4(rgyud): 'gyur ba//yi ge rnams kyis yang dag dkrig/thaMd du ni legs 'phro ba'o//sangs rgyas rnams ni 'dun gnas pa//rab tu thal mo sbyor ba'o//de bzhin byang chub sems dpa' rnams//
12a5(rgyud): rig sngags khro bo'i tshog rnams ni//chos nyan pa yi tshul du yang //dkyil 'khor du ni bzhag par bya//sgo yi phyogs ni thaMd du//sgo skyong ba ni bzhi rnamaso//sbyin pa la sogs ci rigs 
12a6(rgyud): par//bcu drug po'ang bzhag par bya//me tog bdug pa mar me che//ji lta ba bzhin bzhag par bya//lha klu de bzhin gnod sbyin dang //dri za lha min dmyal las byung //'di rnam thaMd lhun po la//kun nas yong+sa 
12a7(rgyud): su bzhag par bya//de nas me tog phreng ldan 'jug/dri dang me tog yang dag ldan//gos dkar 'dzin pa dpal dang ldan//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par rgyan//shes rab 'phro dang lhan cig tu//rdo rje seMs
12a8(rgyud): dpa' rjes dran pa//bdag pas de ni dag par bya//shes rab sbyor bas gang nas ngas//sgo yi drung du legs 'khod la//glu 'di yis ni spyan drang bya//thugs rje mnga' ba chos kyi dbang phyug sangs rgyas yum//
12b1(rgyud): rang gi yab kyi tshogs dang sras dang bcas te byon//spyon cig spyon cig lha dang sring mor bcas mchod pa'i//'jig rten dbang phyug yang dag byang chub thug mnga' ba//spyon cig spyon cig thugs rje'i bdag nyid 
12b2(rgyud): 'jig rten gnyen//chos kyi yi ge bde bar gshegs pas rjes bskos pa//mkha' ltar yongs dag dri med dam bca' dran par mdzod/ma mo rnam ni mchog glus bskul bas byon par 'gyur//de nas bcom 
12b3(rgyud): ldan 'das ma byon par thong nas rang gis me tog gi snyim pa dang mchod yon dang zhabs sil dbul bar bya'o//oM bdag gi mchod yon zhabs bsil 'di//ci bde bar ni bzhesu gsol//de nas de dang lhan 
12b4(rgyud): cig tu//'dres pa ru ni gyur par bsam//oM sarba ta thA ga ta pradz+nyA dz+nyA na sa ma ye hUM sa ma ya staM//de nas 'di yis brtan par bya ste//oM sarba ta thA ga na tsa la sa ma ye ma tsa la s+t+waM/de nas 'dis 
12b5(rgyud): mnyes par bya//oM sarba ta thA gtam na san+tu sti sa ma ye hUM/a h+ye hi dzaHe h+ye hi dzaHde nas dpag tu med pa'i ngo mtshar chen po lta ba'i phyir//oM sarba aM ga puN+Da ri ma hA b+ha ga bAn sa 
12b6(rgyud): le sma ra sma ra s+pha ra s+pha ra sarba a ga a bA yu biSh+Tu ma hA bu d+ha sa mu drAn hUM hUM aM/'di yis ma mo bskul nasu//sangs rgyas sprin ni 'od po che//dkyil 'khor pa la spro bar bya//de nas gsang sngags 'di yi ni//bsngags 
12b7(rgyud): te me tog dor bar bya//oM sarba aM ga pu N+Da ri pra tI ts+tsha d+hi ku su ma aM dza laM hUM aMHde nas ji ltar bshed pa bzhin du dkyil 'khor gyi bum pa rnams la rig sngags 'dis ji ltar bshad pa ltar legs par byin gyis 
12b8(rgyud): brlabs la rang gis dbang skur rang gi snying po rang gis blang bar bya'o//de nas las thaMd kyi nus pa dang /dngos grub thaMd 'grub par 'gyur ro//de nas me tog la sogs dang //bza' dang btung dang ro dag gis//mchod la 
13a1(rgyud): @#//sngon gyi rim pa bzhin//slob ma'i rgyal po gzhug par bya//'khor los bsgyur ba'i rim pa yi//sbyor ba yis ni dbang bskur ro//rdo rje rin chen de bzhin pad//las kyi rdo rje de bzhin no//
13a2(rgyud): zhags pa lcags kyu dril bu dang //lcags sgrog rnga yab dang ni gdugs//ral gri mig sman thur ma gnyis//rgyal po'i nye bar spyod pa dang //de la rgyan rnams brtan nasu//dkyil 'khor der ni dbang bskuro//
13a3(rgyud): rgyal ba'i yum gyis dbang bskur ba//las kun byed pa rab tu bshad//dam tshig gam ni sdom pa yang //rang gi slob ma mnyan du gzhug/zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa'i las//dbang dang drag shul spyod pa ni//
13a4(rgyud): legs par rnam par dag sems kyis//ci 'dod par ni bya ba byos//las rnams kun gyi 'bras bu yang //sbyor ba 'di yis 'grub par 'gyur//de nas bcoM ldan 'das rdo rje sems dpa' chen pos slar
13a5(rgyud): yang las dam pa rgyas par bkod pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//ji lta'i gzugs kyi snod byas la//dkyil 'khor gyis ni
13a6(rgyud): dbusu bzhag/zho dang mar sar 'o ma ni//tsan+dan dmar po dang bsres te//ga bur dang ni ldan byas la//yid la byed pas bzlas te sgrub//zla ba nyi ma gzas bzung tshe//sbyor ba bzang po'i rnam bzhag bya//thog mar me ni
13a7(rgyud): spar byas nas//'bad pa yis ni rlung gi bu//gser gyi snod du bsdu bar bya//ba ru na yo khu bas sbyar//nyi ma dang ni zla ba la//slar yang rdzas de bskam par bya//de nas lho ru lha mo yi//khrag dang sa bon blugs
13a8(rgyud): par bya//de yis myur du rdzas dag ni//'grub par 'gyur te the tsom med//grub nas thog mar lha mo la//cung zad tsam cig dbul bar bya//de bzhin du ni slob dpon la//skal ba nyis 'gyur dbul bar bya//
13b1(rgyud): de nas grub pa gzhan rnams dang //rang gi slob mar skal ba bzhin//de nas byin rlabs sbyor ba yis//phyag rgya dang ni lhan cig tu//rdo rje sems dpa'i ting 'dzin gyis//zas kyis sa bon dang ni bza'//skad cig 
13b2(rgyud): de la gzugs brjes te//rdo rje sems dpa' lta bur gyur//gang dang gang gi gzugs rnams su//sgrub par 'dod pa'i sngags pa yis//de dang de yi bsaM gtan gyis//blo gros brtan pas bza' bar bya//de 
13b3(rgyud): dang de yi gzugs ni//gdon mi za bar brje bar 'gyur//gzhan pa yi ni rdzas dag kyang //'di nyid kyis ni bsgrub par bya//mig sman gyis ni mig byugs pas//khor yug tu ni mi snang 'gro//snying la ldong ros 
13b4(rgyud): kyis byugs na//yid kyi spyod yul nyid du 'gro//ba bla yis ni lus byugs na//mkha' la nye bar 'gro bar 'gyur//mi sman na ga po blangs nas su//nag po'i phyogs la bza' bar bya//
13b5(rgyud): de yi bshang ba'i mar khu las//dud pa mkhas pas bsgrub par bya//de yis mig la byug nas ni//sangs rgyas kyis kyang mi gzigs shing //stobs ni dpal ldan rdo rje'i mtshon//
13b6(rgyud): nyid 'gyur rga dang 'chi ba med//mig sman dang ni ba lang mar//ma he dang ni ra nyid dang //mi yi mig sman nyid dag ni//mkhas pa yis ni blangs nas su//sngags pas gzungs ste btso bar bya//mig sman chen po 
13b7(rgyud): mchog tu bzang //'di yis mig la byug na ni//grub pa yid du 'ong bar 'gyur//mi snang 'grub pa thaMd kyis//blo gros chen po rgyal por 'gyur//sangs rgyas kun gyi sa chen po//nges par thob 'gyur 
13b8(rgyud): gzhan du min//rdzas kyi gnas skabs le'u 'di//mtshungs med chen por nyer bshad do//gos dang gdug sogs ral gri dang //mtshon cha dam pa bsgrub par bya//snying po snying kar byas nas ni//bsgoms 
14a1(rgyud): @#//na myur du 'grub par 'gyur//rdo rje sems dpa' rgyal po che//pad+ma'i sems dpa' bzhin ras mchog/de yi zhal du byas nas su//de dang ldan pas bsgom par bya//mnga' bdag gang gis 
14a2(rgyud): dbang phyug ltar//skad cig de la de 'gyur ro//de nyid kyi ni spyi bo'i char//mchogi dam pa bsam gtan bya//skad cig de la mchod pa che'i//sangs rgyas ni de 'gyur ro//de yi spyi gtsug nyid du ni//sna tshogs rdo rje 
14a3(rgyud): bsgom par bya//de ni kun gyi rgyal dbang po//las kun byed pa'i don grub 'gyur//rdo snying kar bsam par bya//de yi dbus su snying po blta//de nas rdo rje rig pa 'dzin//mnga' bdag rdo rje'i dbang po 'grub//rin 
14a4(rgyud): chen chen po cha brgyad pa//snying ka ru ni mkhas pas sgom//der ni snying po bsam pa yis//rin chen rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur//pad+ma chen po 'dab brgyad pa//yid la bsgoms pas skad cig la//
14a5(rgyud): pad+ma che ldan mnga' bdag gis//pad+ma'i rig pa 'dzin par 'gyur //de la sogs pa mtha' yas pa//ji lta'i sbyor ba'i cho ga yis//mchog dang dman pa 'bring po yi//dngos grub dam pa 
14a6(rgyud): bsgrub par bya//snying kar zla ba'i dkyil 'khor bsam//yi ge dam pa bsgom par bya//ji lta'i sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis//thaMd bdag tu bsgom par bya//de las langs te bdag zhi bas//zla 'od kha ba ltar 
14a7(rgyud): bsil ba'i//'od zer sprin gyis cher gtams pas//bsgrub bya'i spyi bor bzhag par bya//skad cig de la bdugs pa dang //gza' dang dug sogs rab 'phrogs shing //nyi ma gcig gam bdun nam ni//bsgrub

19b8(rgyud): du rgyal ba kun bzhugs nas//bla na med pa'i don mdzad pa//sems can kun la bde slad du//myur du las ni 'grub pa lags//de nas bac+doMs las thaMd kyi dam pa'i dpal zhes bya ba'i ting nge 
20a1(rgyud): 'dzin la snyoms par bzhugs nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi yum gyi dam tshig dam pa de rnams la las kyi dkyil 'khor bka' stsal pa//rlung gi dkyil 'khor 
20a2(rgyud): bri bar bya//de yi dbusu gru gsum pa//de yi steng du lha mo bzhag/'bar ba'i me dang mtshungs pa la//zhal bzhi ma la cher 'jigs pa//mche ba rab tu gtsigs pa mo//thod pa'i phreng ba'i cod pan can//
20a3(rgyud): rdo rje lcags kyu thod pa dang //zhags pa g.yas dang g.yon par 'dzin//lpags pa chen po gosu gyon//khro mo mig 'bras rab phyung ba//mche bas mchu mnan zhal chen mo//de yi mdun du bri bya ba//mtha' 
20a4(rgyud): ma'i zhal ni mdor sdus pa//sna tshogs gtum dang drag pa'i zhal//du ma'i phyag gis yang dag 'khrugs//zhags pa lcags kyu thod sogs dang //rdo rje can gyi sog le 'dzin//thod pas 
20a5(rgyud): lus la bres gyur cing //cod pan chen pos 'jigs byed mo//gshin rje dpa' bos bri bya ba//mtha' gdong dam pa yi ni ma chog/sngon po 'bar ba'i rang bzhin khros//mgo bo'i phreng 
20a6(rgyud): bas rnam par rgyan//thod pa'i phreng ba'i co dpan can//stag gi lpag pa'i sham thabs can//ral gri rin chen thod pa dang //rin chen dbyug gu 'dzin pa'i mchog/de nas lha mo legs bri ba//pad+ma'i 
20a7(rgyud): zhal ma 'jigs chen po//dmar mo phyag na nyi zla 'dzin//bskal pa'i me dang mtshungs pa mo//g.yon par ni khro mo che//ma spyom ma ni ska rags mgul//las kyi rdo rje dri ldan mchog/mche ba rnam par 
20a8(rgyud): gtsigs pa'i zhal//gtsug na thod pa 'jigasu rung //rab gtum stobs dang gsal ba mo//'byor ldan cho ga rig pas bris//phyi rol tu ni legs bri ba//khro bcas 'jigs pa dang bcas pa//
20b1(rgyud): ral pa'i thur tshugs chen po can//khrag gis gang ba'i thod pa dang //phyag rgya bzang pos phyag mtshan pa'o//de yi phyi ru lha rnams ni//snying pa rab sbyar yi dam pa //thams cad du ni yongasu bzhag/
20b2(rgyud): 'jig rten skyong ba bzhag par bya//sgo rnams dag ni thams cad du//sgo skyong ba yang de bzhin du//de nas gtor ma'i cho ga ni//sha bcas btung ba ro dang ldan//de nas go cha bcings gyur pa//thod pa'i
20b3(rgyud): cod pan chen po dang khrag gam chang gis bkang ba 'am//thod pa ldan pas 'jug par bya//thams cad 'grub pa'i ma mo rnams//'di yis spyani drang bar bya//dkon mchogasum la gnod byed pa'i//
20b4(rgyud): gsang ba'i don du ma mo rnams//di ring nyidu dkyil 'khor 'dir//'jigs chen ma rnams thams cad gshegs//'jigs pa'i sgra dang cher gtum dang //kha nas 'od zer stong phrag ni//
20b5(rgyud): mthong ba dang ni thos pa na//mtshan mar yang ni shes par bya//de nas de yis a rag sbyin//ci bder bzhes cig 'jigs mdzad ma//gdug pa chen po gsol bar mdzod//gdug tum
20b6(rgyud): 'jig par byed pa yis//de nas bdag la dbang bskur te//thod par gnas pa'i ro yis ni//srang tsam gyi ni sha zos nas//ma mo thams cad bsgrub par bya//de nas mngon spyod las rnams kun//dam tshig
20b7(rgyud): bsrung ba yis ni bya//dkon mchog gsum la gnod byed dang //bla ma rnams la 'khyal smra ba//rtag tu gsod par lha mo rnams//bgyid par bdag ni rjesu zung //de nas cho ga de nyid kyis//slob ma'i dam pa
20b8(rgyud): gzhug par bya//khro bo'i lha la bskul nasu//me tog dmar po dor bar bya//bdag gi me tog dam pa 'di//ci bde bar ni bzhesu gsol//bdug pa 'di ni bzung nasu//gsol byed 'di ni gsol
21a1(rgyud): @#//bar mdzod//lha mo gang la lhung gyur ba//de yis de la mchog gsolo//de nas dbang bskur byin nasu//dkyil 'khor gyi ni mdun bzhag ste//thod pa ru ni gnas pa yis//khrag 
21a2(rgyud): gis dbang ni bskur bar bya//de nas sgo ru bos nasu//dam tshig 'bad pas 'dzin du gzhug/rab tu gdug pa'i snying gi khrag/bzang po 'di ni 'thung zhig bu//zo shig zo shig sha chen po//btung ba 'jig+sa
21a3(rgyud): pa chen po 'thung //gzhan du na ni 'chi 'gyur ta//mkha' 'gro ma dang gza' rnams dang //gnod sbyin srin po gtum po yi//ring por mi thogs rab tu za//de nas mngon spyod las rnams ni//stong phrag 
21a4(rgyud): dag ni bslab par bya//de yi sbyor ba'i rnal 'byor gyis//rang nyid khro bor bzung gyur nas//gnod byed thams cad bsad par bya//dgra rnams thaMd bza' bar bya//gdug pa thaMd bca' bar 
21a5(rgyud): bya//srin po thaMd gzhom par bya//mtha' yas zhal ma'i sbyor ba yis//bsad dang bzung ba de yis bya//zhal bzhi ma yi sbyor ba yis//thams cad 'gugs pa'i dam dam pa yin//
21a6(rgyud): mkha' ba dong ma yis thams cad du//rin po che yi tshogs 'byungo//pad+ma'i zhal gyi sbyor ba yis//chang gi dam pa btung bar bya//ma spyom ma yi sbyor ba yis//sha ni 'gugs pa'i mchog yin 
21a7(rgyud): no|namakha' rengs pa chen po ste//dmag dpung 'byor pa'i dam pa'o//rmugs dang rmongs pa thams cad du//dpyad pa ru ni mi bya'o//sngar bshad pa yi las rnams dang //ma bshad pa la ci 'dod 
21a8(rgyud): ba|bzlas pa tsam gyis kun bya ste//thams cad du ni the tsom med//dur khrod kyi ni thal ba la//dgra bo gzugs ni 'bad pas bya//ming dang spel bar byas nasu//ma mo'i ral gris btub par bya//
21b1(rgyud): de nas nyi ma bdun na ni//brlab par 'gyur bar gdon mi za//gang lus ji ltar bcad gyur pa//de dang de yi yan lag 'chad//zhal bzhi ma yi sbyor ba yis//sngags pa mnyam par bzhag pas 
21b2(rgyud): bcad//de yi mgrin par zhags pa ni//mkhas pas spros te bcing bar bya//lcags kyus snying khar phug nasu//rlung gis bskyod de drang bar bya//de nas de yi ming bskul nas//de ni shes rab ldan 
21b3(rgyud): pas dgug/dman dang mchog gi dbye ba yis//'di yis 'bras bu nga yis bshad//mdor bsdus pas ni shes bar bya//sngags pa'i rgyal po'i sbyor ba yis sheso//de nas bac+doMs ma de bzhin 
21b4(rgyud): gshegs pa thams cad ni//de yis dngos grub gsol mi 'gyur//nye bar 'tshe ba 'jigs rnams dang //gdon dang gnod sbyin srin po bcas//mi zad nad kyis btab nasu//shi nas 
21b5(rgyud): mnar med nyid du 'gyur //de nas de bzhin dbang bskur gyis//dkyil 'khor la bzhag yum rnams kyis//dbang bskur gsang ba'i dam tshig sbyin//gsang ba'i sgrub thabs 
21b6(rgyud): nyan du gzhug/rang yum ma mo'i sa bon gyis//rang gi rnam rtog bdag yum min//lta bus yab gyur sgrubs na ni//nyi ma bdun na rab tu 'grub//sangs rgyas kun gyi yab ston pa//
21b7(rgyud): kun mkhyen ye shes spyod yul te//drag po kun dang bde chen po//ye shes kun gyi mchog gi mchog/shes rab kyis ni thams cad sgrub//las rgyas pa yi dam pa'i mchog/bye brag tu ni skul nasu//byang chub kyi 
21b8(rgyud): ni yon+tan gyi//thams cad du ni 'od gsal ba'i//dngos grub thams cad bsgrub par bya//shes rab kyi ni las dbye bas//gzugs dang dbyibs nyid dag giso//phyag rgya dang ni phyag rgya'i lan//de bzhin dkyil 'khor dam 
21'a1(rgyud): tshig dang //bza' ba dang ni nyer spyod pa//gtor ma lha bshos nyid dang ni//shin tu bde ba'i stan nyid dang //de dang de ni nges par sbyar//bzlas dang bsam gtan sa bon ni//rang gi lus khyab 
21'a2(rgyud): pa nyid do//gsang sngags dag ni thams cad las//gsang sngags 'di ni rab tu bsngags//tshe 'dir dam pa'i rjes grub cing //gdon mi za bar thob par 'gyur//'di ni bcom ldan kun rdo rje//ye shes 'byung 
21'a3(rgyud): gnas mnga' bdag gsungs//las kun gyi ni sbyor ba yis//'di ni sgyu mas 'gro la bzhugs//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum de rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//
21'a4(rgyud): phyag rgya'i ye shes mchog du dge/ye shes chen pos gsung du gsol//gang gis mngon par rgyas btab kun/mnga' bdag dus mi ring bar 'grub//de nas bac+doMs kyis pa ting nge 'dzin rdo rje zhes
21'a5(rgyud): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//phyag rgya rtagasu yang dag bshad//tha snyad tsam du rnam par brtags//sangs rgyas kun gyi kla klo'i skad//phyag 
21'a6(rgyud): rgya thams cad yang dag bshad//'on kyang mgon po mchog gi brtags//lag pa'i rnam dgod mtshan ma nyid//gang gi yid ches legs skye rnams//dam tshig shes pa'i lha rnams ni//sgrub po rnams kyis 
21'a7(rgyud): rab bskul na/grub pa'i dam pa rab tu stsol//dus las thams cad byon nas su//slar yang gshegs pa nyid du 'gyur//lag pa zla ba nyi mar bya//shes rab thab su rab tu brtag/de nas sngags pas yong+sa 
21'a8(rgyud): su sbyar//rang gi sa bon dag gis mtshan//oM pra dz+nyA pA ya sa ma yo ga/de nas thal mo nges par sbyar//phan tshun rjesu rtogs chen po//khu tshur shin tu bzhag par bya//khu tshur bsdams pa lta bur bcing /
21'b1(rgyud): sau ru ni sbyor bar bya//snying gi dbusu nges par bzung //g.yon pa'i steng du lag pa g.yas//bzhag ste sor mor so sor bya//lagayas sgreng nas phyogs kun tu//'khor lo'i sbyor bas spro bar bya//rdo rje sgo lcag
21'b2(rgyud): lta bur bskum//sor mo thad rtsi bsu bya//oM sa d+harma tsakra sa ma ye//de bzhin g.yon pas yongasu spro//sor mo rtsibs kyi sbyor ba yis//shes rab steng du bsgreng par bya//g.yas pa yang ni de bzhin no//rtse mo cung
21'b3(rgyud): zad sbyar bar bya//chos kyi las ni chen po byed//de nyid snyam du bskums pa ni//las kyi phyag rgya zhesu grags//de bzhin ming med byas nasu//lhag ma rnams ni gdong du sbyar//phyag rgya chen po shin tu ni//
21'b4(rgyud): dngos grub thams cad rab tu gsol//phyag rgya 'di rnams bsdams na ni//rang nyid shes rab bdag por 'gyur//kun tu bzang po rgyal po che//lha dang lha min bcas pas mchod ce'o//de n+sa
21'b5(rgyud): ma mo de rnams kun//slar yang gtso bo la bstod nas//shes rab chen po rnam bzhi yi//phyag rgya zhu ba rnam par byas//phyag rgya bzhi yi rab kyi mchog/bcom ldan 'das kyi bka' stsol cig/
21'b6(rgyud): gsang ba'i phyag rgya'i yal ga mchog/shes rab thabs kyis lus bzang ba//gang zhig gsang ba'i rnal 'byor ni//gang gi rnal 'byor pa bde ba//rdzu 'phrul 'phel dang yang dag pa'i//rdzu 'phrul skye ba
21'b7(rgyud): 'dir rnyed pa//bcom ldan ston pas bka' stsal pa//gsang ba'i phyag rgya rgyal ba'i gzhi//gang gis myur du sgrub po kun//rab tu bde bar gnas pas 'grub//de nas bcom ldan rdo rje can//mnyes
21'b8(rgyud): te gsang ba'i dam pa gsungs//phyag rgya'i pha rol phyin pa rnams//bde ba chen po rab tu sgrub//rdo rje thal mo dam par bsdams//rdo rje dbusu bzhag par bya//gsang ba'i dam tshig zhesu bsgrags//
22a1(rgyud): @#//gsang ba'i rnal 'byor rab tu sgrub//de nyid rdo rje lta bur byas//bskyod pas bde ba bskyed par byed//'di ni chos 'dzin zhes bya ba'i//phyag rgya gsang ba sgrub pa pa'o//g.yon 
22a2(rgyud): par thabs ni bskums nasu//bskyod pas bde ba 'gugs par byed//dag pa'i las zhes rab tu grag/bde ba thams cad rab stsol byed//de nyid kha dang rkan la ni//sbyar ba chen mo zhesu bshad 
22a3(rgyud): rdo rje phan tshun yang dag sbyar//lcags kyu'i kha ni bskyod par bya//kha ni phan tshun yongs sbyar ba//gsing ba'i zhags pa zhesu grags//de bzhin bskums par byas nasu//dam du bsdams te 
22a4(rgyud): bcing bar bya//'di ni gsang ba sdems pa'i rgya//yais sdems pa rab tu 'grub//thams cad rdo rje ltar rdo+e//de bzhin bskyod cing bskor bar bya//de 'di gsang ba rab tshim pa//tshim pa'i don ni ster 
22a5(rgyud): bar byed//lhag ma phyag rgya chen po ru//shes bya phyag rgya mdor bsdus pa'o//de nyid las kyis phyag rgya mchog/las kyi dbye bas sbyar bar bya//zheso//de nas ma mo de rnams 
22a6(rgyud): kun//yid ni tshim zhing mgu ba yis//rdo rje sems dpa' mtshan yangs pa'i//legaso//ba ni nges par phul//legaso legaso gsang chen po//legaso legaso mchod chen po//legaso/leg+sa 
22a7(rgyud): so rnal 'byor che//legaso legaso rab tshim mdzad//de nas bcom ldan 'das ma rnams dang //de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi rdo rje btsun mo rnams kyis dgyes pa'i dkyil 'khor zhu ba bcoM 
22a8(rgyud): ldan 'das la zhus pa//sangs rgyas rang bzhin khyod la 'dud//spyod pa'i rin chen khyod la 'dud//dam pa'i rgyu 'gyur khyod la 'dud//sna tshogs mdzad pa khyod la 'dud//dpa' bo 'dul ba khyod 
22b1(rgyud): la 'dud//gsangs pa'i chos ldan khyod la 'dud//zhi ba'i thugs la phyag 'tshal 'dud//mgon po khyod la bdag cag zhu//bdag gi dkyil 'khor zhu ba'i phyir//mnga' bdag rgyal dang seMn rnams//
22b2(rgyud): de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyis rdo rje btsun mo'i dkyil 'khor bka' stsal pa//de lta'i dkyil 'khor bris nasu//dbusu pha rol phyin ma dgos//d+hI zhes bya ba shes rab 
22b3(rgyud): can||thabs dang ldan pas gsal byas pa//'di yis sngags pa 'jigs med par//zla ba phyed kyis nges par 'gyur//rig pa 'di yis de yis ni//las rnams thams cad byed pa dang //dbang 
22b4(rgyud): dang dgug dang dbye ba dang //tshim dang 'phrog dang 'bras chen po//zhi bas zhi ba zhesu bshad//brnyes ma zhes pas rgyas par bshad//dbang dang dgug dang dbye ba la//byed ma'o dbang la 
22b5(rgyud): drag mo yin//sha yi mtha' ru bdud gsod ma//gsod pa dang ni spel bar bya//'gro ba dang ni yang dag ldan//sor mo zhags pas dkri bar bya//thams cad du ni drag po dgu//drag po 
22b6(rgyud): drag po'i go 'phang gnas//'di yi cho ga ldan pa yis//las kyis tshogs kyi mchog kun bya//'gro ba dang ni yangadag ldan//sor mo zhags bsam te//brgyad rgya kho na bzlas pa yis//dbang bcas nyid dam 
22b7(rgyud): dbang chen po//stobs kyis mgo bo mnan nasu//ma mo'i tshogs kun de yis bzla//dus mi ring bar 'grub par 'gyur//gzi brjid chen pos bka' bzhin du//mgo bos nye bar phyag 'tshal te//
22b8(rgyud): 'di yis ma mo thams cad kun//gzi brjid kyis ni gzi ldan pa//sngags kyi rgyal po 'od ldan pas//go rims ji lta ba bzhin du//dpe zla med pa'i dngos grub byed//grub pa'i lam la rab tu 
23a1(rgyud): @#//gnas||sangs rgyas rdo rje 'dzin pa yi//'phrul dang rnam par 'phrul pa yi//'di ni ston pa chen pos gsungs//ma skyes skye ba med la bzhugs//rtag pa ston pa thaMd pa//nam 
23a2(rgyud): mkha' nyi zla dri ma med//de dag thams cad rab dga' ste//dngos po spangs pa'i btsun mo yis//gar dang glu dang sil snyan mchog/bzhad dang sgeg pa'i sbyor ba dang //bud med sgyu ma'i rnam 'phrul dang //
23a3(rgyud): gling bu pi wang rdza rnga sogs//ting ting shag dang dung sgra dang //ro dang reg pa dri chen po//gzugs dang sgra sogs bde ldan pas//dpa' chen la ni mchod byas nas//snyems dang bcas pas phyag 'tshal te//
23a4(rgyud): slar yang mgon po la gsol btab//rdo rje btsun mo'i dkyil 'khor rnams//mgon po ci yi phyir mi gsungs//yab rnams kyi ni thams cad dang //yum rnams kyi ni mdor bsdus 
23a5(rgyud): pa||mchod pa yang ni thams cad de//bran bcas pho nya pho nya mo//'jig rten pa ni mtha' dag gi/gtso bo chen pos gsung du gsol//de nas bac+dos de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bdag pos 
23a6(rgyud): yang de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd gnyisu med pa rnam par 'phrul pa zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas//de dag nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje la gzhug pa dang //dgug pa dang //
23a7(rgyud): bcings pa dang //mnyes par bya ste/nyid kyi sku dang gsung thugs rdo rje gnyisu med pa bsgyings pa dang //ldan pa la mnyam par gzhag ste gzhugaso//de nas yang bde ba yongasu skyes pa rab tu dgyes 
23a8(rgyud): pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad lhan cig spyod pa dang bcas pa//bran dang bcas pas de la 'di skad ces gsol to//mgon po dbang phyug rdo rje sems//rdo rje snang ba mdzes pa'i sku //gsang 
23b1(rgyud): ba rgyan gyi dkyil 'khor ni//dpa' bo chen pos gsung du gsol//gang gi rgyal ba kun skyed pa//byang chub sems dpa' grags chen po//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyi gasnga
23b2(rgyud): ba rgyan gyi bkod pa dkyil 'khor chen po'i mchog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces gsol to//e ma sangs rgyas thams cad kyis//zhu ba rab tu zhus pa ni//
23b3(rgyud): gang phyir nga la dngos med kyang //zhu ba zhus pa thaMd thos//gsang ba chen po'i smon lam dang //de bzhin snying rje'i pha rol phyin//dran pa med las dran pa yang //dran 'gyur dran po bzhin gnas pa//
23b4(rgyud): sangs rgyas kun gyi gsang chen po//shes rab pha rol phyin dge ba//chos dang chos min rnam par spangs//brjod du med pa bshad par bya//de nas sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das kyi bdag nyid 
23b5(rgyud): de rnams de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gsang ba rgyan gyi dkod pa'i dkyil 'khor gyi rgyal po bka' stsal pa'i phyir//slar yang glu'i dbyangs 'dis zhu ba zhus pa//chos 
23b6(rgyud): dbyings mi g.yo de la legs bzhugs dam pa'i thugs//phyogs bcu mtha' yas sangs rgyas kyis zhabs legs btud pa//bde mchog lha yi lha ste sa/dbang 'jig rten bla//dkyil 'khor 
23b7(rgyud): bde ba mchog la gzhugasol bde chen gyis//sa gnyis gtsug gi phyag byas bde ldan pad+ma'i gtsug tor gyis//rnam pa kun tu gar mdzad sbyor ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa gsungs shig kye//
23b8(rgyud): 'gro kun phan dgongs rab tu gzhug gsol mchod pa chen po kye//cha lugs rnam pa du mas 'gro kun 'dul mdzad kun gyi dam pa kye//de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thaMd kyis 
24a1(rgyud): @#//gsol ba btab cing bskul ba'i tshig nyid kyis thugs la bzhag nas//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor bsgyur ba chen po rab tu sim pa bde ba la 
24a2(rgyud): longs spyod pa rnam par dgyings pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad byin gyis brlabs te//bde ba chen po 'khor los bsgyur ba la rab 
24a3(rgyud): tu gnas nas nyid kyi sku dang gsung dang thugs rdo rje rnams la rab tu phyung ngo//dkyil 'khor snga ma bzhin bris la//de yi lte bar 'di dgod do//rdo chen po bsan+ta pa//'dod pa'i dbang phyug dag dang 
24a4(rgyud): ldan||de bzhin gshegs pa kun gyi yab//kun gyi thog mtha' rab byung ba//de la sogs pa'i rim pa yis//thams cad kun tu brtags par bya//pad+ma'i lo ma rtsibs la ni//rang 
24a5(rgyud): gi chos dang rab tu spel//ma mo rnams ni thams cad kyis//mchod pa la sogs rab tu brtag/sgo ru sgo ba yongs su brtag/sgo yis phyi ru sgo skyong ba//btsun mo lhan cig spyod bcas 
24a6(rgyud): pa||kun nas lha rnams bzhag par bya//de nas rang gi sa bon gyis//klu dang gnod sbyin 'phar ma gzhan//dri za rnams dang mi 'am ci//lha min nam mkha' lding de bzhin//'jig rten skyong ba thaMd ni//
24a7(rgyud): sgo ru gnyis gnyis bzhag par bya//gdon dang dug dang rims dang nad//yi dgas sha za srin po dang //dmyal ba brgyad po phyogs brgyad kyi//'khor lo'i phyi ru bzhag par bya//de nas 'khor lo'i

29b8(rgyud): par brgyan//sngags pas rdo rje bdug pa bri//yid sim byed pa'i dam pa ni//lag na bdug pa mig yangs ma//khur tshos cung zad dkar ba ma'o//lha mo rdo rje snang byed ni//gser bsregs pa yi mdog 'dra la;;;
30a1(rgyud): @#//lag na sgron ma mkhas pas bri//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par brgyan//dri zhim lus la cha lugs bzang //lag na dri yi snon mdzes ma//ut+pal sngon po'i lo ma mtshungs//mdzes pI 
30a2(rgyud): gzugs la yid 'phrog ma'o//gzugs kyi rdo rje mdzes pa'i gzugs//rgyan rnams kun gyis rnal par brgyan//lag na rdo rje me long 'dzin//'dzum bcas dgod pa'i dkyil 'khor can//sgra yi rdo rje sna tshogs 
30a3(rgyud): lus||dkar dang sngo ba'i lus dang ldan//pi wa nga gling bu ting ting sogs//sgrogs pa shes rab can gyis bris//ro yi rdo rje skal chen mo//lag na ro yi snod 'dzin pa//rgyan rnams thaMd yang dag rdzogs//
30a4(rgyud): mtshan nyid kun dang yang dag ldan//rdo rje reg bya sngo sangs che//chu skyes sngon po 'dab 'dra'i mig/rnam par gsal zhing cher dgod pa//rang gi dpung pa bsdams po mo//bzhad pa'i rdo rje bong bcas pa//

30a6(rgyud): gnyis bzhag cing //mdzes pa'i phreng ba'i chun po btsug/rdo rje glu mkhan legs dmar lus//glu len pa la rab tu brtson//chu skyes dmar po rgyas 'dra'i bzhin//glu len lag pa rab tu mdzes//rdo rje gar 
30a7(rgyud): ma mkhas pas bri//gar dang bcas pa'i lag pa can//sngo bsangs dkar po cher dkar ba//mig che ba la 'dzum bcas mchog/rdo rje sil snyan drag mo che//dur du ra yi rnga mdzes rdung //rdo rje rnga chen lha mo 
30a8(rgyud): che//rnga chen rdung ba bri bar bya//rdza rnga ma ni mkhas pas bri//rdza rnga rdung bar byed pa mo//lha mo mu kuN+Da rdung ma//mu kuN+Da ma bzhin mdzes ma'o//pa Ta ha ni rdung byed ma//rdo rje pa Ta ha zhes bya//rdza rnga chen po rdung byed 
30b1(rgyud): ma;rdo rje rdza rnga mdzes ma bri//guny+dza rdung byed dga' ba mo//dgod bcas pad+ma'i mig can ma//rdo rje guny+dza dpa' bos bri//lus kun gyi ni dam pa 'grub//lag na rdo rje ting ting shag/rdo rje ting ting shag bri bya//

30b4(rgyud): la rab spro ba'o//rdo rje 'bud dung 'bad pas bri//dung dkar 'bud pa'i rang bzhin can//dung ma lag na zangs dung 'dzin//'dod pa'i gzugs mchog 'dzin pa mo//rgyal ba'i dril bus 
30b5(rgyud): lag pa mdzes//khrol ba yang dag bri bar bya//leg spro ba la gzi brjid che//rnam par rgyal la spro byed ma'o//pad+ma'i gar gyi dbang phyug dmar//pad+ma dmar po kha 'byed ma//gar gyi rgyu mas rab gnas 
30b6(rgyud): pa'i//lag pa stong ldan bri bar byas//rin chen gar ma lha mo che//gar gyi bdag nyid legs par bri//lag na rin chen snyems pa can//lag du ma gdong brgya pa'o//rdo rje gar ma bsnyems bcas ma//

30b8(rgyud): sna tshogs sgyu ma legs sems ma//smin ma bsgyur ba'i mig mdzes ma//spos bcas gdong gi rnam 'gyur can//mthong na dga' ma 'dzum pa'i bzhin//gdong gi dkyil 'khor sngo bsangs mchog/

31b8(rgyud): des ni slar yang gsol ba gdab//yang dang yang du bskul bar bya//lha yi rnal 'byor de nyid kyis//nga rgyal yang dang yang byas te//mchod pa shin tu chen po bya//des nas mchod yon zhabs bsil yang //gasnga 
32a1(rgyud): ba'i de nyid kyis bskul te//sngags kyis de nyid rig pas dbul//sangs rgyas kun bdag phyag na rdo rje ni//mdzes pa'i shes rab kyis 'khyud sbyor ldan pas//yab yum sras bcas 'khor bcas

35a6(rgyud): phyag rgya sngags pas shes par bya/ro 'phrog pa yi las rnamaso//de ni gnyisu byas nasu//g.yas pa 'am ni g.yon pa yis//snying gar legs sbyar sbyor bdagis//reg pa'i phyag rgya ngo mtshar che//'di yis chos 
35a7(rgyud): dang dam tshig dang //las kyang rab tu skye ba yin//cho ga shes pas rnal 'byor sbyar//reg pas 'bebs pa'i mchog yin no//de bzhin sbyar bas so sor bya//g.yon pa rked par bzhag par bya//g.yas pa kha yi

35b1(rgyud): yang khar//mkhas pas dal gyis bskyod par bya//de gnyis snying khar yongasu sbyar//bsnyems pas bdud dang 'gyed par bya//ji srid spyi bor gtugs pa yis//chos dang la sogs kun tu 'gyur//snying po la sogs sngags
35b2(rgyud): rig pas//phyag rgya 'di yis rgyas btab pa//dngos grub thaMd thob 'gyur te//rdo rje sems dpa' bka' bzhin no//thal mo mnyam por legs par sbyar//g.yon pa'i steng du de bzhin gzhan//de dag snying khar bsgreng

35b4(rgyud): g.yon par bzhag par bya//'od ldan rdo rje'i gdong pa dang //mdor sdus nas ni sbyar nasu//g.yas pa thur du 'phyang bar bya//de bzhin na steng bstan mchog sbyin no//me tog snyim pa yang dag sbyar//rdo rje gnyis

35b6(rgyud): bya//g.yon pa snying kar yongasu bzhag/g.yas pa spro ba'i sbyor ba yis//yongasu skor zhing bskor nasu//g.yas pa ru ni bzhag par bya//rdo rje thal mo brkyang nasu//rdo rje'i steng du 'od 'dzes ma//phyi rol bskul ba'i sbyor
35b7(rgyud): ba yis//de yi steng du gdong pa zhag/phyag rgya 'di ni phyag rgya che//brtson 'grus pha rol phyin dge ma'o//de bzhin lag pa steng du sbyar//rtsibs mchog rin chen ma ru 'gyur//lhag ma'i bya ba de nyid kyis//de bzhin

36a1(rgyud): @#//kyu'i rnam pa yis//bltas nas lus can 'gugs par byed//de las sor mo'i rtse mo rnams//cung cad snol te bskyang bar bya//rdo rje mche ba lta bur sgreng //kha sbyar sgrol byed ngo mtshar

36a3(rgyud): le'i thal mo nges par ni//'dir ni thod pa nyid du 'gyur//'di ni gnyis pa nyid yin te//las kun gyi ni dam pa sgrub//de bzhin bcing ba legs bcings nas//rab tu sbyin pa brkyang bar bya//g.yas pa steng du

36a8(rgyud): kyis|sngar bshad pa yi phyag rgya nyid//thod pa rdo rje'ang shes par bya//phan tshun gcig la gcig tu ni//thaMd gzungs kyi phyag rgya ru//dkyil 'khor du ni rab tu bsgrags//'di rnams sbyor ba ji lta bar//

36b4(rgyud): rnams legs tshim nas//rdo rje can la gsol ba ni//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dbus gnas par//rdo rje dkyil 'khor bsgom par bya//de yi steng du rdo rje che//rdo rje la ni dang po mchog/nyi ma gser mdog dang 
36b5(rgyud): mtshungs pa//gsang sngags shes pas de bsgom bya//rang gi lus las spros nasu//rang gi rnam par bsam par bya//de nas sngags bdag 'grub par 'gyur//sngags kyi de nyid bdag 
36b6(rgyud): po cher//kun tu 'gro ba sna tshogs che//rdo rje sems dpa' 'dra ba bzhin//gnyis pa'i de nyid sgom pa ni//dkyil 'khor pad+ma las byung ba//dmar po'i gos la gzi brjid che//'bar ba dag ni me dang mtshungs 
36b7(rgyud): rang las spro byas thaMd du/'jig rten dbang phyug lta bur 'gyur//dkyil 'khor dbang chen bzhag par bya//dkyil 'khor la ni cher dge ma//dang po'i lha mo drod dang ldan//rang gi lus la nges par khyab //
36b8(rgyud): bsdus nas bdag dang 'dra ba'i bdag/yid bzhin nor bu'i snang bar 'gyur//rlung gi dkyil 'khor dbus son par//'gro ba'i sgo gtan lha mo ni//rang lus lus ldan thaMd du//ljang ku dang ni ljang ser ma//
37a1(rgyud): @#//las kun sgrub pa'i dam pa yis//rdo 'dzin pa dang ldan 'gyur//ma mo bzhi dang yang dag ldan//shes rab ye shes dam pa gnas//dkyil 'khor kun gyi dam pa ru //mkhas pas yis ni bsgom par

37a3(rgyud): bdag por bdag 'gyur ro//zhes bcom ldan 'das kun tu bzang pos bka' stsal to//gsang ba rgyan gyi bkod pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po las//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yum gyi phyag rgya'i ye shes

40a1(rgyud): @#//par 'gyur//rgyal bas zhu ba 'di dag gis//phreng ba can la zhus zheso//de nas bac+doMs kun tu bzang po chen pos zhu ba thaMd rnam par spyod pa long spyod pa rdo rje zhes bya ba'i tinge 'dzin la snyoms

40a3(rgyud): bag chags sbyor ba dang ldan bas//gnyi ga dag ni gdon yin te//grol dang ma grol zhesu bshad//de ni rnal 'byor pa ru brjod//gsang sngags yid du bshad pa ste//snying po yang ni de bzhin no//yid la rnam par bzhag

40a8(rgyud): mnyam sbyor bas//tsum pa na zhes nges par bshad//gnas pas rtags zhes bshad pa ste//rtags ni lus zhes brjod pa yin//de yi 'khyud pa reg par shes//chags pa mthong ba'i phyag rgya'o//lag pa'i 'od

100b1(rgyud): thogs par bri//de yi 'og tu lha mo bri//rdo rje'i rtse mo stobs chen te//pho nya rnams kyi yongs bskor cing //bzhin ni bag tsam 'dzum par bri//g.yon rol du ni sngags pa yis//rdo rje lu gu rgyud ces pa//lag na rdo rje 
100b2(rgyud): lu gu rgyud//rang gi pho nya rnams kyis bskor//pri yang ku ltar ljang ser mdog/rdo rje'i mtshan mas legs mtshan bri//lag na rdo rje'i 'og logs su//khro zla gtso bo zhes bya ba//bgegs chen dgra ni zhi phyed pa//
100b3(rgyud): khro bo 'jig rten gsum du grags//mig gsum mche ba bzhi pa ste//kha dog dbyar gyi char sprin 'dra//rab tu rgod pa'i sgra yang 'byin//rdo rje'i rgyan gyis rnam par brgyan//'khor ni mtha' yas khro bo yis//dbang 
100b4(rgyud): phyug rnams kyis yongs bskor ba//lag pa stong phrag brgya dag ni//mtshon cha sna tshogs thogs shing gdengs//khro bo chen po 'di 'dra ba//pad+ma'i steng na 'dug par bri//de yi nub tu song nas ni//rdo rje 
100b5(rgyud): 'dzin pa mang po bri//lag na tshon cha sna tshogs thogs//kha dog sna tshogs sa stobs po che//'od zer dag gis yongs bskor ba//seMn kun don byed pa bri//gsang sngags mgon po'i 'og logs su//
100b6(rgyud): bden bral gyi ni phyogs kyi char//mi g.yo zhes bya lag na ni//ral gri bzhags pa legs par thogs//zur pud spyi bo g.yon du 'phyang //rab tu rgyan cing mig gcig yo//rang gi 'od kyi nang na gnas//khro dang 
100b7(rgyud): bcas pa brag la 'dug/bzhin ni khro gnyer rab tu brnyer//byis pa'i gzugs can mkhrang bag can//bris nas blo ldan rlung phyogs su//song nas bcom ldan 'das kyi na//khro bo 'jig rten gsum las 
100b8(rgyud): rgyal//'bar ba'i phreng can mi zad pa//lag na rdo rje rtse bran rgyan//bka' lung nod pa ltar 'dug cing //zad pa'i dus dang 'drar gnas pa//nan+tan nyid du bri bar bya//de ltar nang du bri ba ni//rgyan dang bcas 
101a1(rgyud): par bshad pa yin//de nas sngags pa phyir byung nas//nges par gnyis pa'i cha dag la//shar gyi phyogs kyi nang du ni//cha ni gnyisu phye ba la//sgo khyud kyi ni nang rol du//sngags pas 
101a2(rgyud): rgyal ba shAkya thub//gser gyi kha dog 'bar dang bcas//mtshan mchog sum cu rtsa gnyis ldan//mgon po na bza' dur smig banabs//khyab bdag bstan pa'i don mdzad pa//pad+ma dkar po'i stan la bzhug+sa//
101a3(rgyud): chos 'chad tshul du bzhugs par bri//de yi g.yasu lha mo ni//sangs rgyas spyan zhes bya ba ste//lha mo cung zad 'dzum pa'i zhal//'od kyi dkyil 'khor 'dom gang pa//mtshungs med sku ni rab tu dangs//de ni 
101a4(rgyud): shAkya thub pa'i yum//de yi g.yasu lha mo ni //rigs sngags ma dzod spu zhes grags pa//pad+ma'i steng na 'dug pa ste//dung zla kun da lta bu'i mdog/lag na yid bzhin nor bu thogs//re ba kun 
101a5(rgyud): rdzogs byed pa bri//de nas sngags pas g.yon rol tu//shAkya seng+ge skyob pa yi//gtsug tor gzi can brtson 'grus che//gdugs dkar po dang rgyal ba'i gtsug/gtsug tor rnam par rgyal 
101a6(rgyud): nyid dang //gzi brjid phung po skal chen dang //rnam par mthor zhes rnam grags pa//lnga po yang dag bri bar bya//shAkya thub ni mthu chen gyi//gtsug tor lnga po 'di dag ni//gnas rnams lnga rnams nyid du ni//
101a7(rgyud): rab tu bsgrims te bri bar bya//de yi byang du song nas su//gnas gtsang rnams ni bri bar bya//dbang phyug kun tu me tog dang //'od kyi phreng ba nyid dang ni//yid mgyogs dang ni rnam grangs te//de dag thaMd legs 
101a8(rgyud): par bri//mdzod spu yi ni g.yas logasu//gtsug tor chen po cher 'byung ba//de bzhin gyen du 'byung nyid dang //sgra dbyangs mtha' yas pa yang ste//gtsug tor gsum po kho na ni//bsgrim+sa te yang dag bri bar bya//
101b1(rgyud): gtsug tor lnga po stobs can rnams//dkar po ser skya ser por bya//mthu chen gtsug tor lnga po yi//gsum po gzhan ni dkar po dang //ser po dang ni dmar por bya//kun kyang 'od zer mang po can//rgyan rnams 
101b2(rgyud): kun gyis brgyan pa yin//de dag smon lam stobs chen de//re ba thaMd rdzogs byed pa'o//de nas sngags pas ma phyogasu//me ni drang srong che rnams dang //bcas shing me yi nang na 'dug/thal ba'i sol ris gsum gyis
101b3(rgyud): mtshan//dpa' bo thaMd rab tu dmar//snying ka gsum mdo ris mtshan can//me lce 'bar ba'i dbus na 'dug/bgrang phreng gun d+he thogs par bri//de nas lho yi phyogs kyi char//gshin rje gtso bo lag pa na//
101b4(rgyud): dbyug pa thogs shing ma he zhon//kha dog dbyar gyi char sprin 'dra//ma mo rnams dang dus mtshan dang //'chi bdag rnams kyis bskor bar bri//bden bral phyogasu bden bral ni//lag na 
101b5(rgyud): ral gri 'dzin pa bri//de nas kyang ni blo ldan pa//myur du byang gi phyogs song ste//lha yi rgyal po ri rab la//'dug cing lha rnams kyis bskor ba//lag na rdo rje cod pan can//rgyan rnams kun gyis
101b6(rgyud): brgyan par bri//de nas de yi shar du ni//chu lha lag na zhags thogs bri//de yi yang ni shar phyogasu//nyi ma shing rta'i steng 'dug bri//de yi g.yas dang g.yon du ni//rgyal dang rnam par rgyal ba bri//de yi g.yasu 
101b7(rgyud): tshangs pa ni//gdong bzhi pa la thor tshugs can//oM zhes bya ba lhur len pa//ngang pa pad+ma'i stan 'dug 'dri//de yi nub tu bri ba ni//sa yi lha dang dbyangs can dang //khyab 'jug skem byed de bzhin rlung //bde byed
101b8(rgyud): de bzhin zla ba ste//nub kyi phyogasu gnas nas ni//rab tu nan tan bya ste bri//bris nas sngags pas de nas ni//yid la 'khrul pa med pa ru//gsang sngags mgon po'i 'og logasu//khro chen gzhan 
102a1(rgyud): @#//gyi mi thub ces//bya ba nyid dang lha mo ni//gzhan gyis mi thub ma dang ni//sa bdag lag na bum pa thogs//gus par yang dag gnas par bri//klu chen dga' bo bsnyen dga' dag/
102a2(rgyud): shAkya seng+ge skyob pa yi//sgo drung gnyisu mngon phyogs par//rab tu bsgrims te bri bar bya//gzhan yang shAkya thub pa yis//rgyud rnams kun las dkyil 'khor du//sngags dang phyag rgyas gang bshad pa//
102a3(rgyud): de dag kyang ni mkhas pas bri//sngags pa de nas phyir byung nas//cha gsum pa ni nges pa la//rdo rje dam pa sbyin pa ni//gur gum lta bu'i mdog can de//spyi bor zur phud lnga dang ldan//byis pa gzhun nu'i gzugs
102a4(rgyud): can la;;ut+pal sngon po rdo rje can//lag pa g.yon na thogs pa ste//pad+ma dkar po'i stan la 'dug/rab tu mdzum ba bzhin du gnas//'od zer mtshan nyid bzang po yis//kun tu 'khor bar 
102a5(rgyud): sngags pas bri//de yi byang du gzhun nu ni//dra ba'i 'od can lag pa na//rin chen dra ba thogs pa ste//sna tshogs pa yi rgyan gyis brgyan//pad+ma dmar po'i stan la 'dug/rgyal ba'i sras la

102a7(rgyud): du;;phyag brnyan ma ni lnga rnams dang //gzhon nu dra ba'i 'od can gyi//'khor gnyis po yang bri bar bya//de nas sngags pas lho phyogasu//byang chub sems dpa' grags chen po//sgrib pa thaMd rnam sel 
102a8(rgyud): gtso;;yid bzhin nor bu thogs par bri//de yi cha gnyis bor nas ni//dpa' bo ngo mtshar can dang ni//sems can kun la mi 'jigs sbyin//ngan song thaMd spong ba dang //de bzhin skyong sems blo gros
102b1(rgyud): dang;;blo can snying rjes gdungs pa dang //byams pa sngon du 'byung ba dang //gdung ba thaMd zhi byed dang //de bzhin blo gros bsam yas te//mkhas pa brgyad po bri bar bya//byang gi phyogasu song nas ni//
102b2(rgyud): sa yi snying po 'bar dang bcas//bzod dang des pa phun tshogs bri//de yi stan ni mkhas pa yis//'byung khung kun dang ldan pa yis//rin chen kun gyi nyer rgyan pa//de yi steng na rin chen bzhi'i//pad+ma yang
102b3(rgyud): dag gnas pa yi//yid du 'ong ba bsam bya ste//byang chub sems dpa' grags chen po//de yi steng na 'dug par bri//de yi 'khor ni yon+tan sgo//mtha' yas shing ni gang+sa med de//de yi nang nas byang chub sems//
102b4(rgyud): rin chen 'byung gnas rin chen lag/sa 'dzin nyid dang lag dang ni//rin chen phyag rgya can dang ni//lhag pa'i bsam pa brtan pa nyid //grags chen 'di dag bri bar bya//de bzhin sngags pas nub
102b5(rgyud): phyogasu//dpa' bo namakha'i snying po ni//gzi brjid chen po gos dkar gyon//lag na ral gri thogs bsam ste//rgyal sras pad+ma'i stan steng na//legs par 'dug pa bri bar bya//bdag nyid chen po

102b7(rgyud): bri zhing //rtag tu dpa' bo 'od rnams dang //lhan cig go rims bzhin du bsam//de nas gsang ba pa'i bdag po lag na rdo rjes thaMd dang ldan pa'i 'khor rnams la bltas nas//bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang la

103a1(rgyud): @#//na mi 'byung ngo//gsang sngags spyod pa'i tshul 'byung ba//de bas kyang ni dkon pa ste//bskal pa bye ba du ma ru//sdig pa bgyid par gyur pa gang //de kun dkyil 'khor 'di 'dra ba//
103a2(rgyud): mthong bas byang bar 'gyur lags na//grags pa mtha' yas sngags spyad pa'i//tshul la gnas na smos ci dgos//skyob pa'i gsang sngags bzlas nas ni//go 'phang bla na med par 'gyur//gang dag 
103a3(rgyud): spyad pa mchog 'di la//blo gras shin tu ma g.yo ba//de dag gis ni ngan 'gro'i rgyun//sdug bsngal kun 'byung rab tu bcad//de'i tshe thaMd dang ldan pa'i 'khor de dag dang //rdo rje 'dzin de dag gis mgrin gcig

103a7(rgyud): tshad dang ni//sgo yi tshad kyang ci 'drar bgyi//mgon po yis ni bshad du gsol//lha bshos me tog bdug spos dang //bum pa rnaMs kyang rgyas par gsungs//slob ma ji ltar gzud pa dang //de ni ci ltar

103b5(rgyud): ni snying po yi//dkyil 'khor dang ni mnyam zhes bya//'jigs med dag gis rim gyis ni//bskyed de de bas lha shes bya//sgo rnams kyi ni bar bar du//sgo srung rnams ni bzhag par bya//ting nge 
103b6(rgyud): 'dzin ni mdor na sems//yang dag mgu ba yin par bshad//sems dpa' chen po khyad par ni//yid gcig bsdus la mnyan par bgyis//sangs rgyas kun gyi ting 'dzin ni//stong pa nyid ces

104a1(rgyud): @#//rang sangs rgyas rnams dang //byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin so so'i tshul gsungaso//de'i tshe yang sangs rgyas thaMd dang rgyud gcig pa'i stobs kyi shugs zhes 
104a2(rgyud): bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugaso//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis byang chub sems dpa' phyag na rdo rje la bka' stsal pa/byang chub snying por 'dug nasu//bdud ni rnam pa bzhi bcom

105b1(rgyud): par las//las tshe rnam par sbyong zhes bya //gsang ba pa'i bdag po gzhan yang //gsang sngags rnams kyi mtshan nyid ni//sangs rgyas rnams kyis ma byas//byed du ma bcug/rjesu yi rang ba ma yin no//de ci'i phyir
105b2(rgyud): zhe na༐ 'di ni chos rnams kyi chos nyid do//de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa rnams byung yang rung /ma byung yang rung //chos rnams kyi chos nyid de ni ye nas gnas pa ste/de yang 'di ltar gsang sngags rnams
105b3(rgyud): kyi gsang sngags kyi chos nyid do//gsang ba pa'i bdag po de la gang dag gi tshe yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thaMd mkhyen pa /thaMd gzigs pa 'jig rten du byung ba de'i tshe/chos nyid de tshul sna
105b4(rgyud): tshogs dang /dgong+sa pa sna tshogs kyis sems can bsam pa sna tshogs pa rnams la tshig       sna tshogs dang //yig ༐ 'bru sna tshogs dang /yul gyi nges pa'i tshig tu brjod pa sna tshogs 
105b5(rgyud): dang ༐ dbyangs sna tshogs su go ba rnamasubyin gyis brlabs te gsang sngags kyis tshul 'chad do//ji ltar na de bzhin gshegs pa rnamasagasang sngags kyi tshul yig 'bru dag tu byin gyis rlob

111b1(rgyud): na maHsa man+ta bud+d+hA nAM/a sa me tri sa me sa ma ye swA hA/de nas de ma thag tu sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thaMd du dam tshig la 'jug pa'i rig sngags kyi 
111b2(rgyud): sgra gang zhig thos na mkhas pa rnams chos thaMd las 'da' bar mi 'gyur ba'i sgra grags par gyur to//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das kyis chos kyi dbyings kun tu 'byung ba'i gsang sngags gsungs pa/na maH
111b3(rgyud): sa man+ta bud+d+hA nA/d+harma d+hA tu swa b+hA ba At+ma ko ho/chos kyi dbyingasu byin gyis brlabs pa'o//na maHsa man+ta badzrA nAn/badzra At+ma ko ho/rdo rje sems par byin gyis brlab pa'o//na maHsa man+ta bradz+nyA nAn/badzra ka ba 
111b4(rgyud): tsa hU~M/rdo rje go cha'o//na maHsa man+ta bud+d+ha nAn/ta thA ga ta tsak+Shu/d+h+ya a ba lo ka ya swA hA//de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyan no//na maHsa man+ta bud d+ha nAn/bi shud d+ha gan d+haud b+ha ba swA hA/byug spos 
111b5(rgyud): kyi sngagaso//na maHsa man+ta bud+d+d+ha nAM/ma hA me tri byud ga te swA hA//me tog gi sngagaso//na maHsa man+tabud d+hA nAn/d+harma d+hA tu a nu ga ta swA hA//bdug spos kyi sngagaso//na maHsa man sta bud d+hA nAn/
111b6(rgyud): sarba ta thA ga ta ra tsi spa ra Na a ba b+hA sa ga ga not+ta re b+h+yaHswA hA//mar me'i sngagaso//na maHsa man+tabad d+hA nAn/ga ga na sa ma sa ma swA hA//mchod yon gyi sngagaso//na maHsa man+ta/bud+d+hA nAM/ga ga na nan d+ha s+pha ra Na/bi shud 
111b7(rgyud): d+ha d+harma ni ra dza ta swA hA//gtsug tor gyi sngagaso//na maHsa ma n+ta bud d+hA nAn/pra tsa n+d+ra badzra dz+wa la pi s+phu ra hU~M/de bzhin gshegs pa'i go cha'o//na maHsa man+ta bud d+hA nAn/dz+wA la mA li na ta thA ga ta ra tsi swA hA//kun tu 
111b8(rgyud): 'bar ba'o//na maHsa man+ta bud+d+ha nAn/ma hA ta thA ga ta dzi hwa sad s+ya/d+harma pra ti Sh+Thi ta swA/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ljags kyi'o//dkyil 'khor du dgod pa'i gsang sngags kyi mdzodo////
112a1(rgyud): @#//bam po gsum pa//de nas yang bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la phyag na rdo rjes zhus pa/bcom ldan 'das ji ltar na/dkyil 'khor 'dri ba'i tshe na ni//bgeg+sa 
112a2(rgyud): rnams zhi bar 'gyur ba lags//gsang sngags sgrub pa'i tshe na yang //log 'dren rnams ni ma mchis 'gyur//gsang sngags rnams kyang ji ltar sgrub//de yi 'bras bu ci 'drar 'gyur//de skad gsol nas de 
112a3(rgyud): yi tshe//bcom ldan rnam snang mdzad chen gyis//'di skad ces ni bka' stsal pa//sems dpa' chen po legaso legs//khyod kyis yongasu dris pa gang //thaMd khyod la bltan par bya//sngon chad ser 
112a4(rgyud): sna bsten pa'i phyir//bgeg sna rang gi sems las byung //de yi rgyu ni gzhag pa'i phyir//gang du sems dang sems byung ba'i//rtog pa thaMd spong ba dang //rnam par rtog pa las byung ba'i//spos pa 
112a5(rgyud): thaMd 'gag 'gyur ba'i//byang chub sems zhes bshad pa yin//sngags pas byang chub sems dran zhing //mi g.yo ba ni yid la bzlas//de yi phyag rgya bcings na ni//bgeg+sa rnams thaMd 'jig 
112a6(rgyud): par 'gyur//gsang ba pa'i bdag po de la//rlung bcing ba 'di nyon cig/a zhes bya bar bdag bsgyur te//haM zhes bya ba yid la bzlas//rlung gi phyogasu gnas nas ni//sa la spos kyi thig le dag/
112a7(rgyud): bdun nyid legs par byas nasu//kham phor gyis ni dgab par bya//kham phor ri rab chen po ru//yang dang yang du bsam par bya//de yi steng du a zhes pa//klad kor bcas pa rgyas par bsam//rlung 
112a8(rgyud): bcing chen po de lta bu//sngon gyi rgyal ba rnams kyis bshad//sems dpa' chen po chu yi yang //yid gcig bsdud te mnyan par gyis//sngags pas ra zhes bya ba ni//thaMd dmar po stobs chen bsaM//
112b1(rgyud): 'bar ba'i phreng can mi zad pa//khro bo lag na ral gri thogs//sa la sprin gyi gzugs bris nas//ral gri phyag rgyas dbab par bya//phyogs kun nas ni langs pa'i sprin//de ma thag tu 'jig par 'gyur//yang
112b2(rgyud): na sngags pa mi g.yeng bas//de dag phur bus gdab bya ste//mkhas pa rdo rjer gyur nasu//thams cad rdo rje phur bus gdab//yang na bgegs rnams thaMd ni//zhi bar bya ba yang bshad de//sngags pa dkyil
112b3(rgyud): 'khor dbus gnas nas//mi g.yo stobs chen gsang sngags ni//shin tu mi zad bdag nyid kyi//dkyil 'khor dag la gnas par bsam//de yi gzugs brnyan byas nas ni//rkang pa g.yon pas spyi bor
112b4(rgyud): gzir//'on tang de ni zhir 'gyur ba 'am//dogs pa med par 'chi bar 'gyur//de yi gzugs brnyan byas//skye tshe dug khrag bsres pa yis//byugs pas sngags pas bskams na ni//gdon de myur
112b5(rgyud): du tshig par 'gyur//de ni me la bskams pa dang //dug gis bskus na nges par ni//tshangs pa 'am 'on te brgya byin yang //de yi mod la myur du 'tshig/de nas yang bcom ldan 'das la phyag
112b6(rgyud): na rdo rjes 'dis skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa'i don bdag gis 'tshal ba ltar na bdag gis 'di ltar mjal to//bcom ldan 'das rang gi dkyil 'khor la gnas na lha rnams
112b7(rgyud): byin gyis rlob par bgyi do//de nyid du gnas na de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' las kyang 'gal bar mi bgyid do//de ci'i slad du zhe na//bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar rang gi rigs la gnas pa de ni gsang
112b8(rgyud): sngags thaMd kyi dam tshig lagaso//byang chub sems dpa' gsang sngags kyi sgo nas byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa rnams kyis kyang rang gi nas la gnas nas/don thaMd bsgrub par bya'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis
113a1(rgyud): @#//bka' stsal pa//gsang ba pa'i bdag po de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa de de bzhin no//gsang ba pa'i bdag pa gzhan yang gang gi kha dog gang gsungs pa/de nyid de yi dkyil 'khor

113a3(rgyud): gnyis dang som nyi mang ba/thos pa tsam snying por 'dzin pa/sgrub pa la mi phyog pa dag 'byung bar 'gyur te//de dag ni bdag nyid kyang ma rung la gzhan yang phung bar byed pa yin no//'di skad du 'di ni phyi 
113a4(rgyud): rol pa rnams la yod de/sangs rgyas kyis gsungs pa ni ma yi no//zhes smra bar 'gyur gyi mi blun po de dag gis 'di ltar bcom ldan 'das thaMd mkhyen pa/chos thaMd la mnga' brnyas pa/seMn 
113a5(rgyud): gyi don rtogs par thugasu chud pa des sngon sems can rnams la phan par bzhed nas 'di dag thaMd bshad do zhes bya bar ni mi sheso//chos rnams kyi ni chos kyi mtshan//blun po des

113a7(rgyud): de nas phyag na rdo rje la sogs pa rdo rje 'dzin de dag dang /kun tu bzang po la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' de rnams kyis bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad la phyag 'tshal nas/snying rje chen po

113b1(rgyud): ba btab bo//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyis byang chub sems dpa' de dag dang /rdo rje 'dzin de dag la mi nyams pa'i chos nyid du byin gyis brlabs nas bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi
